Method: 开发了CommuniWave机器学习模型，整合了基于mmaction2的行为捕捉网络(BCN)、自主研发的YOLOv10模型(YLX)以及使用随机森林的行为评估模型(BEM)，从街景视频中生成非正式行为程度(DIB)波动图表。

Result: 该模型能够高效检测和量化城市社区中的非正式行为程度(DIB)，实现动态监测，为城市管理者提供决策支持。

Conclusion: CommuniWave模型通过量化非正式行为为社区规划提供数据支撑，有助于城市管理者做出精细化决策，从而提升社区整体韧性。

Abstract: For urban managers and designers, improving the functional attributes of urban communities to enhance territorial resilience in the face of complexity and uncertainty is crucial. Currently, community planning often follows a top-down approach and lacks effective metrics to quantify informal behaviors of residents, leading to frequent conflicts with original plans. This study introduces CommuniWave, a machine learning model designed to efficiently detect and quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB) in urban communities. The model integrates a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a self-developed YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest. Ultimately, by generating DIB fluctuation charts from street videos, the model facilitates dynamic monitoring, supporting urban managers in making refined decisions to enhance the overall resilience of communities.

</details>


### [145] [SHAP-Weighted Cross-Modal Expert Fusion for Emotion and Sentiment Recognition: Evidence and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08573)
*Adis Alihodzic,Selma Skopljakovic Hubljar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析SHAP归约方式对专家维度差异的影响，发现sum-abs归约能保留高维跨模态专家的贡献，使XGAF方法在多模态情感识别任务中达到与早融合相当的性能，同时保持了模块化和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感识别方法存在权衡：早融合准确但缺乏模块化，晚融合模块化但可能丢失跨模态交互。XGAF方法通过TreeSHAP归因幅度自适应组合单模态和跨模态专家，但当专家特征维度不均等时，SHAP归约方式会影响跨模态专家的权重分配，需要深入分析这一现象。

Method: 研究XGAF框架下不同SHAP归约方式（mean-abs、median-abs、sum-abs）对专家权重的影响，特别关注专家维度不均等的情况。在MELD数据集（7类情感识别）和CMU-MOSEI数据集（3类情感识别）上，使用三种人脸序列聚合器（包括Transformer）进行实验，并通过McNemar检验和消融实验分析各组件贡献。

Result: 在MELD上，sum-abs XGAF达到0.5983 F1分数，与早融合（0.6018）无显著差异（p=1.000），显著优于晚融合（0.4598，p<0.0001）。在CMU-MOSEI上，sum-abs XGAF达到0.6519，略超早融合（0.6485）和晚融合（0.5696）。消融实验显示主要增益来自跨模态专家（特别是三模态专家），而非复杂样本路由。诊断表明mean-abs和median-abs权重近似均匀分布，而sum-abs权重集中在三模态专家上。

Conclusion: SHAP归约方式对模块化多模态融合有重要影响：sum-abs归约能保留高维跨模态专家的总归因质量，避免其被压制。跨模态专家的引入是性能提升的关键，XGAF方法成功地在保持模块化和可解释性的同时，达到了与早融合相当的识别精度。该研究为可解释多模态融合提供了透明的实证分析框架。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition is commonly addressed by early fusion, which concatenates modalities before classification, or late fusion, which combines independently trained unimodal predictors. Early fusion can be accurate but monolithic, while late fusion is modular but may lose cross-modal interactions. This paper revisits XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), a tree-based mixture of unimodal and cross-modal experts whose sample-level weights are derived from TreeSHAP attribution magnitudes. We focus on the effect of SHAP attribution reduction when experts have unequal feature dimensionalities. In this setting, mean-abs and median-abs reductions can suppress high-dimensional cross-modal experts, whereas sum-abs reduction preserves total attribution mass. On MELD 7-class emotion recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion across three face-sequence aggregators; the Transformer variant reaches 0.5983 \wf{}, compared with 0.6018 for early fusion and 0.4598 for probability-average late fusion. McNemar testing shows no significant difference between sum-abs \xgaf{} and early fusion on MELD ($p=1.000$), while \xgaf{} remains significantly better than late fusion ($p<0.0001$). On CMU-MOSEI 3-class sentiment recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} reaches 0.6519 \wf{}, slightly exceeding early fusion (0.6485) and late fusion (0.5696). Ablation studies show that the main gain comes from adding cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal expert, rather than from complex per-sample routing. Diagnostics further show that mean-abs and median-abs weights are nearly uniform, while sum-abs weights concentrate on the trimodal expert. Thus, the main contribution is a transparent empirical analysis of how SHAP reduction, expert dimensionality, and cross-modal expert design affect modular multimodal fusion.

</details>


### [146] [Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08602)
*Peng Cui,Jitao Wang,Siyan Xue,Yao Huang,Haoming Xia,Dong Li,Dengxiang Liu,Weilin Wang,Liping Liu,Leida Zhang,Yunfu Cui,Tao Peng,Daolin Ji,Haitao Zhao,Wei Zhang,Xiaojuan Wang,Weijie Ma,Zongren Ding,Jinlong Li,Yuan Ding,Jiajing Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Chengkun Yang,Ziyue Huang,Jiaqi Liu,Fusheng Liu,Yang Zhou,Xiaojuan Wang,Zhongquan Sun,Shiyun Bao,Xiaojun Wang,Ming Yang,Guangxin Li,Bin Shu,Yong Liao,Hongxuan Li,Yao Tang,Shizhong Yang,Yongyi Zeng,Yufeng Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Jihui Hao,Jun Zhu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HCC-STAR，一个面向肝细胞癌（HCC）的临床对齐大语言模型，能够基于电子病历叙事文本联合输出风险分层、指南一致的治疗推荐及个体化生存预测，在多中心队列中表现优于现有临床指南及竞争模型，辅助医生实现更精准、高效的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的HCC临床指南和分期系统分类较粗糙，经常忽略分期内的异质性及电子病历中的临床背景信息，导致个性化治疗和预后评估不足。

Method: 研究者从SEER数据库整理约30,000例HCC病例，通过临床医生验证的提示增强工作流将其扩展为EMR风格的叙述性训练数据。在此基础上，开发了知识对齐的推理框架，采用步骤可验证的复合奖励优化，超越对临床指南的文本级记忆，实现对EMR叙事的深度理解与推理。

Result: 在中国12家医院6,668名患者的多中心队列中，HCC-STAR在治疗推荐和风险分层方面达到最先进水平，优于BCLC、CNLC指南及GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro等竞争模型。假设性总生存分析显示，遵循HCC-STAR推荐的中位生存期为51个月，而BCLC和CNLC分别为29和32个月。在临床医生中心评估中，盲法肝胆专家认为HCC-STAR的推理和证据支撑可信；模型在治疗准确性上超越住院医师和主治医师，并作为助手帮助医生更快、更准确地制定决策。

Conclusion: HCC-STAR是一个可靠且可验证的决策支持系统，能够有效改善HCC患者的风险分层和精准治疗，有望在临床实践中提升诊疗质量和患者预后。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

</details>


### [147] [The complexities of patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08625)
*João Matos,Olivia Buege,Donny Cheung,Gary S. Collins,Paula Dhiman,Nan Li,Bingyu Mao,Benjamin W. Nelson,Michail Ouroutzoglou,Paul Varghese,Jonathan Amar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析真实医患对话数据，开发了能模拟多样化患者沟通风格的仿真系统，发现沟通方式会显著影响LLM的分诊性能，强调AI系统需适应现实的沟通多样性以避免加剧健康不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前面向消费者的健康聊天机器人开发和评估主要依赖于配合度高、表达清晰的模拟患者，忽视了真实用户在沟通模式和情感表达上的多样性，这可能导致系统在现实部署中表现不佳并加剧健康差距。

Method: 1. 分析2,053个真实患者-聊天机器人对话，识别沟通模式和情感表达的差异；2. 开发独立建模临床内容、情感状态、对话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器；3. 采用图灵测试式的评估方法，由15名人类评分员判断对话真实性；4. 使用5种不同患者角色和1,164个临床评分病例，评估4个LLM在紧急程度评估中的表现。

Result: 1. 在真实性评估中，人类评分员区分真实与模拟对话的准确率仅为55%，模拟对话几乎与真实对话难以区分；2. 沟通风格会显著改变分诊结果；3. 为理想化而非现实交互设计的系统存在表现不佳和放大健康差距的风险。

Conclusion: 以患者为中心的对话式人工智能必须适应沟通多样性。系统设计应基于真实而非理想化的交互场景，否则在实际部署中将面临性能下降和加剧健康不平等的风险。

Abstract: Consumer-facing health chatbots powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for symptom assessment. However, chatbot development and evaluation often rely on cooperative, articulate, simulated patients. We analysed 2,053 real patient-chatbot conversations and found that communication patterns and expression of emotions vary widely across users. We developed a patient simulator that separately models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation of realism with 15 human graders, simulated conversations were nearly indistinguishable from real ones, with human graders achieving an accuracy of 55%. We used five distinct patient personae, across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, to evaluate the performance of four LLMs in urgency assessment. We found that communication style can significantly alter triage outcomes. Patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence must accommodate communication diversity: systems designed for idealised, rather than realistic, interactions risk underperforming and amplifying health disparities when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [148] [Formal Mechanisms for Market Stability in Self-Interested Agent Societies: A Marketplace Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08652)
*Eugene Ng Yi Sheng,Bingquan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在不受限通信基础上叠加何种形式机制能够维持自利Agent社会的市场稳定。实验发现Mediation机制表现最佳，即使面对优化攻击，市场也能保持正向效用，实现"可弯曲但不可折断"的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自利Agent在重复社会困境中倾向于背叛，导致合作收益崩溃。现有研究缺乏对叠加在不受限通信之上的形式化机制及其对抗攻击鲁棒性的系统研究。本文旨在探究何种机制能够维持Agent社会的市场稳定，以及这些机制对对抗攻击的抵抗能力。

Method: 构建多Agent市场仿真环境，18个DeepSeek-V3 LLM Agent具有互补生产专长，在受限社会网络中进行交易。实验分为两阶段：(1)比较8种机制条件，在200轮中逐步注入troll，识别最优机制Mediation；(2)对Mediation进行对抗红队测试，使用迭代提示优化的LLM驱动troll进行攻击。

Result: Mediation机制表现最佳。最优攻击(v6)使诚实Agent效用降低13.3%，但无法使市场崩溃。Mediation在持续对抗压力下仍能实现恢复。Mediation机制具备对抗鲁棒性：可弯曲但不可折断。

Conclusion: Mediation机制能够有效维持自利Agent社会的市场稳定，在面对优化的对抗攻击时展现出强鲁棒性。形式化机制可以叠加在不受限通信之上保障合作收益，为多Agent系统设计提供了理论与实践参考。

Abstract: Self-interested agents, left unconstrained, tend toward defection in repeated social dilemmas, causing cooperative gains from trade to collapse. This paper investigates what formal mechanisms, layered on top of unrestricted communication, are sufficient for a society of such agents to maintain market stability, and how resilient those mechanisms are to adversarial attack. We instantiate the research question as a multi-agent marketplace simulation where 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) with complementary production specialties must trade within a constrained social network to obtain utility. We conduct two experimental phases: (1) a mechanism comparison across eight conditions under progressive troll injection over 200 rounds, identifying Mediation as the top-performing mechanism; and (2) adversarial red-teaming of Mediation using iteratively prompt-optimised LLM-driven trolls, finding that the best attack (v6) reduces honest-agent utility by 13.3% but cannot collapse the market. Mediation enables recovery even under sustained adversarial pressure. We define adversarial robustness as a mechanism's ability to sustain positive honest-agent utility under optimised attack, and find that Mediation is robust: it can be bent but not broken.

</details>


### [149] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SolarChain-Eval，一个用于评估去中心化能源市场中可信经济智能体的物理约束基准，发现RL智能体存在效用-安全性权衡，LLM Planner/Auditor能改善可审计性但无法弥补奖励函数设计缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI系统在信息物理环境中的应用增加，需要同时评估任务性能和可信度。在去中心化能源市场中，自主智能体虽然能提高市场效用，但可能利用无效物理数据、创造人为流动性并产生不稳定的治理决策，因此需要专门的评估框架。

Method: 提出了SolarChain-Eval基准，将市场治理建模为Gymnasium兼容的马尔可夫决策过程。智能体每小时做出决策，系统从市场效用、物理安全性、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性和可审计性多个维度评估策略。引入基于LLM的Planner/Auditor层，Planner定义回合级动作边界和审计规则，Auditor审查和修正高风险动作，并通过结构化日志记录所有干预。

Result: 实验（静态、随机、短视、RL和RL+LLM策略）揭示了明显的效用-安全性权衡。RL智能体提高市场效用但仍可能产生不安全行为；移除物理惩罚后，奖励最大化智能体会利用无效发电并增加人为流动性。LLM Planner/Auditor改善了可审计性并缓解了部分风险，但无法完全补偿错误指定的奖励函数。

Conclusion: 可信智能体AI评估需要同时具备物理约束和透明的干预追踪，单纯依赖LLM层无法弥补奖励函数设计的缺陷。研究已开放数据和代码以确保可复现性。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


### [150] [Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08716)
*Yifan Wu,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Bo Peng,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动记忆干预机制，通过独立的记忆代理来缓解长程任务中的"行为状态衰减"问题，在Terminal-Bench和τ²-Bench上分别提升了8.3pp和6.8pp的pass@1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程任务中，决策相关的状态信息往往分散在不断扩展的轨迹中，随着轨迹增长，任务需求、环境事实、先前尝试、诊断信息和开放子目标等信息可能被埋在上下文窗口中或被推到窗口之外，导致在需要时无法影响决策。作者将这种失效模式称为"行为状态衰减"（behavioral state decay）。

Method: 作者将记忆视为主动干预机制而非被动检索。一个独立的记忆代理与未修改的行动代理并行运行，从近期轨迹更新结构化记忆库，并决定是否注入基于记忆的提醒或保持静默。该模块是即插即用的，可与前沿行动代理和现有的代理框架配合使用。此外，作者还通过SFT和GRPO在SETA上训练Qwen3.5-27B，作为向开放权重记忆策略迈出的早期步骤。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0和τ²-Bench上，该方法对较弱和较强的行动代理都能提高pass@1性能，在Terminal-Bench上提升+8.3 pp，在τ²-Bench上提升+6.8 pp。消融实验表明，选择性干预优于被动记忆库暴露、持续注入、仅顾问指导和通用检索。训练的Qwen3.5-27B模型改善了验证奖励，并实现对Terminal-Bench的部分迁移。

Conclusion: 将记忆作为主动干预机制而非被动检索是有效的，选择性干预策略在长程任务中显著优于其他替代方案，该方法为开放权重记忆策略的发展提供了有价值的早期探索。

Abstract: In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $τ^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $τ^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.

</details>


### [151] [The Illusion of Equivalency: Statistical Characterization of Quantization Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08734)
*Baha Rababah,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Carson K. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 传统量化评估指标存在缺陷，作者提出正确性一致性指标发现量化会导致行为差异，并分析了注意力权重的结构敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练后量化评估仅依赖准确率和困惑度，无法捕捉量化引起的行为变化，需要新的评估方法来揭示基线模型与量化模型之间的真实差异。

Method: 提出正确性一致性这一决策级指标，衡量基线模型与量化模型正确预测的重叠程度；将量化建模为注意力权重的结构算子，使用统计和分布度量量化层级失真。

Result: 在中等量化程度下，即使任务性能保持不变，也会出现行为分歧；查询和键投影比价值和输出投影更敏感；低位宽存在非线性断点。

Conclusion: 基线模型与量化模型之间的等价性是一种错觉，需要在传统性能指标之外进行行为评估。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is widely used to deploy large language models in resource-constrained settings, yet its evaluation relies almost exclusively on accuracy and perplexity. We show that these metrics fail to capture behavioral changes induced by quantization. We introduce correctness agreement, a decision-level metric that measures overlap in correct predictions between a base model and its quantized variants, independent of absolute accuracy. Across multiple models and quantization schemes from 8-bit to 2-bit, we find that behavioral divergence emerges under moderate quantization even when task performance appears preserved. To explain this effect, we analyze quantization as a structural operator on attention weights and quantify layer-wise distortions using statistical and distributional measures. Our results reveal non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and show that query and key projections are consistently more sensitive than value and output projections. These findings expose an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models and motivate behavioral evaluation beyond conventional performance metrics.

</details>


### [152] [Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08740)
*Emanuele Quinto,Carlo Andrea Rozzi,Francesco Zanitti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受Lisp启发但语言无关的概念模型，用于大语言模型工作流系统，核心贡献是引入了语义持久化的概念，将工作流定义、实例、推理记录等都表示为可持久化的知识对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型应用日益广泛，这些应用越来越多地使用显式工作流来实现工具使用、检索、分支、检查点和人工审批等功能。虽然现有工作流系统已经解决了很多执行层面的问题，但缺乏一个统一的概念模型来解释和持久化这些工作流相关的知识对象。

Method: 提出了一种受Lisp启发但与语言无关的概念模型，该模型采用三个解释性视角：符号形式、对象同一性和活图像思维。在这个模型中，工作流定义、工作流实例、推理记录、上下文快照和依赖关系都被表示为共享知识基底中的持久化知识对象。核心语义区分在于derive（确定性计算）和infer（中介LLM判断）这两个概念。

Result: 得到了语义持久化的初步概念框架：工作流不仅能产生知识并留下痕迹，而且可以自身被表示为可检查、可恢复和可审查的知识对象，同时保持形式化转换语义作为未来工作方向。

Conclusion: 该概念模型为LLM工作流系统提供了一个新的理论基础，使得工作流可以作为知识对象进行持久化存储和管理，增强了工作流的可观察性、可恢复性和可审计性，为后续的形式化语义研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy.
  The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.

</details>


### [153] [AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08745)
*Siddharth Damodharan,Radhika Gupta,Ali Alshami,Ryan Rabinowitz,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AUTOPILOT-VQA，一个面向行车记录仪视频理解的、以交通事故为中心的视觉问答基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在安全关键场景下的可靠推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型、大型语言模型和多模态大型语言模型在自动驾驶任务上取得了进展，但评估这些模型是否能对安全关键事件进行可靠推理仍然具有挑战性。现有评估体系缺乏针对真实驾驶事故和险情的标准化基准。

Method: 构建了一个以事故为中心的视觉问答基准数据集AUTOPILOT-VQA，包含围绕真实驾驶事故和险情设计的结构化问题。基准涵盖多种安全相关类别：天气与照明条件、交通环境、道路布局、路面状态、标志牌、涉及实体、事故发生、碰撞位置及可避免性推理，要求模型对场景上下文和事件级细节进行回答。

Result: 成功构建了涵盖多维度安全相关类别的标准化基准，迫使模型超越目标识别，转向时序定位与安全感知推理。该数据集已作为CVPR 2026 AUTOPIOT竞赛的一部分发布。

Conclusion: AUTOPILOT-VQA为评估自动驾驶系统在不同场景下的可靠性提供了标准化基准，将推动更具可解释性、鲁棒性和安全意识的视觉语言系统在实际自动驾驶中的应用与发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [154] [Using AI-based Learning Assistants in Higher Education: A Large-Scale Descriptive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08748)
*Kristina Schaaff,Quintus Stierstorfer,Valerie Heckel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于77,543名学生的客观数据，大规模分析了AI学习助手在高等教育中的实际使用模式，发现该工具已融入学生日常学习，但使用行为在人口统计学和结构因素上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育聊天机器人研究主要依赖小样本和自我报告的问卷调查数据，缺乏大规模的实际使用行为证据。研究旨在填补这一空白，为AI学习支持系统的进一步发展提供实证基础。

Method: 大规模描述性分析方法。基于77,543名远程教育学生的客观日志数据，考察了不同性别、年龄段、学习集群、学位类型和学习模式下AI学习助手（Syntea）的使用模式。

Result: Syntea已融入许多学习者的日常学习惯例中，但使用情况在不同的人口统计学背景（如性别、年龄）和结构背景（如学位、学习模式）下存在差异。研究识别出了这些具体的使用模式。

Conclusion: 本研究识别了AI学习助手在不同人群中的使用模式，为AI学习支持的进一步发展提供了实证基础，贡献了高等教育中教育聊天机器人使用的大规模分析，弥补了现有研究的局限性。

Abstract: In this study, we present a large-scale descriptive analysis of the use of an AI-based learning assistant (Syntea) in higher education. Based on objective log data from 77,543 students enrolled in distance studies, we examine usage patterns across gender, age group, study cluster, degree, and study mode. To date, existing research on educational chatbots has largely relied on comparatively small samples and self-reported survey data, while large-scale evidence on actual usage behavior remains limited. Our findings show that Syntea is already embedded in the study routines of many learners, but that usage differs across demographic and structural contexts. By identifying these patterns, our study provides an empirical basis for the further development of AI-based learning support and contributes a large-scale analysis of educational chatbot usage in higher education.

</details>


### [155] [Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08758)
*Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Yan Li,Donggang Li,Xiru Hu,Hokin Deng,Ziyang Gong,Xuanyi Zhou,Huacan Wang,Xiangchao Yan,Wanghan Xu,Wenlong Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Yue Zhou,Yifan Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了IdeaGene-Bench基准，用于评估AI系统在科学谱系推理和基于谱系的想法生成能力，发现现有最强系统在谱系推理任务上准确率仅为27.3%，揭示了组合推理瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学思想的产生遵循继承结构，类似于生物基因组（继承机制、修复已知局限、重组早期工作片段），但现有基准缺乏对AI系统是否能够遵循这种继承结构的评估能力。

Method: 构建IdeaGene框架，将每篇论文表示为最小化、类型化、证据支撑的Idea Genome对象集合；设计GenomeDiff记录六种操作进化动力学下的继承、突变、丢失、外部导入和新插入；创建包含1,961条黄金谱系轨迹、1,085个Idea Genome对象和920条GenomeDiff记录的数据集；设计IG-Exam评估封闭式谱系推理能力和IG-Arena评估基于谱系条件的研究提案生成能力。

Result: 在14个基于LLM的科学助手系统上进行实验，发现存在组合推理瓶颈：最强系统在谱系推理任务上仅达到27.3%的精确准确率；结构化谱系上下文改变了系统排名，而非对所有参与者提供均等帮助。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在科学谱系推理能力上存在显著局限，需要进一步研究以提升AI系统理解和遵循科学思想继承结构的能力；结构化谱系上下文的有效性因系统而异。

Abstract: Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [AI-Driven Thermal Mapping and Management in 3D Integrated Photonic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07711)
*Liton Kumar Biswas,Katayoon Yahyaei,Shajib Ghosh,M Shafkat M Khan,Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Rayhane Ghane-Motlagh,Mahdi Nikdast,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出结合AI模型和启发式方法的混合方案，解决了3D光子集成电路热管理中稀疏传感器数据难以生成完整热分布图的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维光子集成电路（3D PICs）因高密度键合和异质材料引入了关键的热管理挑战。传统方法如热显微镜和封装内传感器只能提供稀疏数据，限制了完整热分布的可视化能力，难以实现有效的热管理。

Method: 提出双方法解决方案：(1) AI驱动热建模框架：整合稀疏传感器数据与设计层、密度信息，预测多层温度变化；(2) 启发式方法：利用局部材料属性、设计布局、组件几何形状和传感器坐标，通过设计区域间的比较分析调整局部热阻率，细化特定区域的热估算。通过对传感器数据进行插值并调整局部热阻率生成3D PIC的二维热图。

Result: 成功生成3D PIC的二维热图，启发式方法有效补充AI模型，在不依赖大量训练数据的情况下提高了估算精度。两种方法协同工作，实现了可扩展、高精度的热映射解决方案。

Conclusion: 该混合方法为下一代3D光子系统的实时热映射和设计时仿真提供了可扩展、准确的解决方案，能够实现可靠的热管理，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Photonic Integrated Circuits (PICs) are advancing high-performance computing, data centers, and sensing, yet three-dimensional (3D) PICs introduce critical thermal management challenges due to high-density bonding and heterogeneous materials. Traditional methods like thermal microscopes and in-package sensors yield sparse data, limiting full thermal profile visibility. This paper presents a dual-method solution combining an AI-driven thermal modeling framework with a design-based heuristic approach. The AI method integrates sparse sensor data with design layer and density information to predict multilayer temperature variations, while the heuristic approach uses localized material properties, design layout, component geometries, and sensor coordinates to refine thermal estimations in specific regions. A 2D thermal map of a 3D PIC is generated by interpolating sensor data and adjusting for local thermal resistivity using comparative analysis between design regions. The heuristic method complements the AI model, improving estimation accuracy without extensive training data. Together, these methods offer a scalable, accurate solution for real-time thermal mapping and design-time simulation, enabling reliable thermal management in next-generation 3D photonic systems.

</details>


### [157] [A Theoretical Framework for Stochastic Activity Prediction in Tensor Accelerator Wallace-Tree Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08002)
*Prashanthi Metku,Chandra Gandu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出随机活动预测（SAP）技术，通过检查操作数的汉明重量来预测低开关活动并冻结输入，从而减少张量加速器乘法器在稀疏操作数情况下的动态功耗，同时保证计算正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量加速器乘法器在每个时钟周期都消耗动态功率，即使稀疏操作数需要很少的内部开关。现有技术无法解决此问题：零检测需要精确为零的操作数，结构性功率门控需要空闲乘法器，离线权重选择无法响应运行时数据。

Method: 引入随机活动预测（SAP）技术，在乘法器执行前检查到达操作数的汉明重量，预测低开关活动，并在确定性安全控制器独立确认重用正确时冻结输入。该方法基于三个形式化结果：(i)谱收缩引理证明Wallace树活动取决于操作数位密度而非位位置；(ii)信息保留定理表明每周期一位能捕获O(n²)内部节点的几乎所有预测信息；(iii)伯努利最优性定理证明所选编码在汉明重量统计的校准一位编码器家族中是最优的。

Result: 建立了Lipschitz常数Lφ=3/2，在256周期窗口中预测误差低于10^-13；信息保留率η_I ≥ 1 - O(log n/n)，证明每周期一位编码几乎捕获所有预测信息；错误预测仅导致节省损失，从不产生错误答案。

Conclusion: SAP填补了张量加速器功耗堆栈中现有技术未覆盖的特定层次，为稀疏操作数场景下的动态功耗优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor accelerator multipliers burn dynamic power on every clock cycle, even when sparse operands require very little internal switching. No existing technique addresses this: zero-detection requires exactly-zero operands, structural power gating requires an idle multiplier, and offline weight selection cannot respond to runtime data. This paper introduces Stochastic Activity Prediction (SAP), which closes this gap by examining the Hamming weight of arriving operands before the multiplier executes, predicting low switching activity, and freezing the inputs when a deterministic Safety Controller independently confirms the reuse is correct. Mispredictions cause missed savings, never wrong answers. Three formal results underpin SAP: (i) a Spectral Contraction Lemma proving that Wallace-tree activity depends on operand bit density, not bit position, establishing Lipschitz constant $Lφ= 3/2$ and prediction error below $10^{-13}$ for a 256-cycle window; (ii) an Information Retention Theorem showing $η_I \ge 1 - O(\log n/n)$, so one bit per cycle captures nearly all predictive information about $O(n^2)$ internal nodes; and (iii) a Bernoulli Optimality Theorem proving the chosen encoding is shown to be optimal, within the family of calibrated one-bit encoders of Hamming-weight statistics considered. SAP addresses the specific layer of the tensor accelerator power stack that existing techniques do not cover.

</details>


### [158] [CRIMP: Compact & Reliable DNN Inference on In-Memory Processing via Crossbar-Aligned Compression and Non-ideality Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08015)
*Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Xiangzhong Luo,Shiqing Li,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的训练框架，同时解决了基于交叉棒的内存处理加速器面临的量化、剪枝和非理想特性三大问题，实现了无硬件开销的高精度模型部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于交叉棒的内存处理加速器面临三个关键障碍：1)浮点运算与交叉棒不兼容，现有量化方案仍需浮点处理器处理缩放因子；2)冗余参数占用过多交叉棒资源，现有剪枝方法需数据对齐导致额外开销；3)写入变化等非理想特性降低模型精度，现有补偿方法开销巨大。这些问题单独解决会引入额外开销，亟需统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出三种协同优化方法：1)重用交叉棒中的位移单元近似计算缩放因子，避免浮点处理器；2)采用核分组剪枝和交叉棒剪枝移除数据对齐所需的硬件单元；3)利用交叉棒特性实现运行时感知的非理想特性自适应，从训练阶段缓解器件非理想性的影响。将三种优化集成到一个统一的训练框架中。

Result: 实验表明：量化带来的精度损失可忽略不计；剪枝方法相比最先进方法实现了更高的稀疏度和精度；生成的VGG-16和ResNet-56模型在CIFAR-10上仅分别产生2.19%和1.26%的精度下降，且无额外硬件开销。

Conclusion: 通过将量化、剪枝和非理想特性补偿整合到统一训练框架中，有效降低了训练开销并提升了精度，为IMP加速器提供了一种高效、实用的整数型、剪枝且可靠的模型部署方案。

Abstract: Crossbar-based In-Memory Processing (IMP) accelerators achieve high-speed, low-power computing for deep neural networks (DNNs), but face three obstacles. First, floating-point (FP) arithmetic is incompatible with crossbars, and existing quantization schemes still require FP processors for scaling factors, incurring hardware overhead. Second, redundant DNN parameters occupy too many crossbars, and current IMP-aware pruning methods require data aligning across crossbars, introducing significant memory and computing overhead. Third, non-ideal crossbar behaviors such as write variations degrade the accuracy of deployed models, and existing compensation methods add substantial overhead. In this paper, we address all three problems within a single training process. We reuse bit-shift units in crossbars to approximately multiply scaling factors, avoiding FP processors. We apply kernel-group pruning and crossbar pruning to remove the hardware units needed for data aligning. We adopt runtime-aware non-ideality adaptation to relieve the impact of device non-ideality from the training stage by exploiting crossbar features. Integrating these three optimizations into one comprehensive learning framework reduces training overhead and improves accuracy. Experiments show that our quantization incurs a negligible accuracy drop, and our pruning achieves higher sparsity and accuracy than state-of-the-art methods. Our framework produces integer-only, pruned, and reliable VGG-16 and ResNet-56 models for CIFAR-10 on IMP accelerators, with accuracy drops of only 2.19% and 1.26%, respectively, without hardware overhead.

</details>


### [159] [Who Needs DRAM? We Have Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08407)
*Hannah Atmer,Thiemo Voigt,Yuan Yao,Stefanos Kaxiras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fiber Memory架构，利用光纤作为可循环延迟线存储器来存储LLM权重等不可变数据，在10,000个AI加速器上消除了冗余权重存储，并将权重传输能耗降低70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI对高性能内存的巨大需求以及超大规模数据中心的扩张，给DRAM的可用性和合约价格带来了巨大压力。现有的DRAM存储方案在应对大规模AI工作负载时面临存储冗余和能耗高的问题，亟需新的架构来解决这些挑战。

Method: 提出Fiber Memory架构，将光纤重新定位为主动的循环延迟线存储器。采用数据并行光广播延迟线存储架构，结合空分复用多芯光纤(MCFs)、无源光抽头放大接口、共封装光学(CPO)和区域全光再生技术，专门用于存储LLM权重等不可变数据。

Result: 案例研究评估表明，Fiber Memory架构能够在10,000个AI加速器中消除冗余权重存储，相比传统的HBM3e配置，权重传输能耗降低超过70%。

Conclusion: Fiber Memory为超大规模数据中心提供了一种创新的内存解决方案，通过重新利用光纤技术来存储不可变数据，有效缓解了DRAM压力，显著降低了能耗，为生成式AI的高性能内存需求提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: The rising pressure on DRAM availability and contract pricing reflects generative AI's massive high-performance memory requirements. This pressure is heavily compounded by hyperscale data center expansion, which now consumes a significant portion of global DRAM output. In this work, we propose a new architecture: Fiber Memory, which reimagines the role of optical fiber in a hyperscale data center, deploying it as an active, recirculating delay-line memory for immutable data, such as large language model (LLM) weights. We present a data-parallel optical broadcast delay-line memory architecture that accounts for fiber's physical realities. By incorporating space-division multiplexed multi-core fibers (MCFs), passive optical tap-and-amplify interfaces, co-packaged optics (CPO), and regional all-optical regeneration, our case study evaluation demonstrates that Fiber Memory can eliminate redundant weight storage across 10,000 AI accelerators and reduce weight-delivery energy by over 70% compared to traditional HBM3e configurations.

</details>


### [160] [FPGN: Redefining Ultra-Fast Programmable Gate-based Neural Acceleration with Differentiable LUTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08427)
*Jiawei Liang,Haotong Qin,Linfeng Du,Xingyu Liu,Shangkun Li,Hui Yu,Michele Magno,Xinyu Chen,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了FPGN框架，实现了LUT原生神经网络在FPGA上的低延迟部署，相比传统方法获得最高205倍延迟降低和30倍LUT效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPGA加速器以算术为中心，未充分利用LUT的逻辑表达能力；而现有LUT原生神经网络仅停留在算法层面，缺乏与FPGA硬件原语的真实匹配、物理感知的拓扑设计以及自动化优化流程，无法实现理论潜力到高性能硬件的转化。

Method: 提出FPGN端到端物理感知框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)硬件对齐的可微分LUT神经元训练方法；(2)结构化LUT原生拓扑与流式硬件架构，改善布线局部性和时序收敛；(3)延迟驱动编译器，利用高保真度分析性QoR模型实现自动化设计空间探索和硬件生成。

Result: 实验表明FPGN相比代表性FPGA-BNN加速器实现最高205倍延迟降低，相比先前可微分LUT原生网络实现最高30倍LUT效率提升，同时保持有竞争力的推理精度。

Conclusion: FPGN成功弥合了LUT原生学习与延迟优化FPGA实现之间的鸿沟，证明了硬件感知设计对于释放LUT原生神经网络性能潜力的重要性。

Abstract: Achieving nanosecond-scale inference latency for deep neural networks (DNNs) has become a primary architectural concern for latency-critical applications. While Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs) offer a promising substrate for low-latency inference, conventional FPGA accelerators remain arithmetic-centric, using LUTs primarily as building blocks for numerical operators and peripheral logic. In contrast, recent LUT-native neural networks treat LUTs as learnable neurons, revealing promising theoretical potential to exploit their intrinsic logic expressivity. However, existing methods are largely confined to algorithmic optimizations, failing to translate this theoretical potential into high-performance FPGA accelerators. Specifically, their differentiable formulations do not faithfully match FPGA LUT primitives, their physically-unaware topologies compromise routability and timing closure, and their lack of automated optimization flow hinders systematic design space exploration (DSE) and efficient hardware implementation.
  In this paper, we propose FPGN, an end-to-end physically-aware framework that closes the gap between LUT-native learning and latency-optimized FPGA implementation. FPGN addresses these challenges through (i) a hardware-aligned differentiable formulation for training FPGA-native LUT neurons, (ii) a structured LUT-native topology with a streaming hardware architecture to improve routing locality and timing closure, and (iii) a latency-driven compiler that leverages high-fidelity analytical Quality of Results models to automate DSE and hardware generation. Experiments show that FPGN achieves up to 205$\times$ latency reduction compared to representative FPGA-based BNN accelerators and up to 30$\times$ higher LUT efficiency than prior differentiable LUT-native networks, while maintaining competitive inference accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [CTA-Pipelining: A Latency-Oriented Spatial Scaling Method for Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07862)
*Tingkai Liu,Muralidhar Andoorveedu,Sanjoy Das,Sanjay Patel,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CTA-pipelining执行范式，利用共享内存多GPU系统特性，在8卡H200/B200上将MLP操作延迟相比微批处理降低31.8%，相比张量并行降低29.6%，且可与TP正交结合进一步降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多GPU系统已演变为紧密集成的共享内存结构，但现有软件仍将其视为高速网络；同时大语言模型服务对延迟约束的需求使GPU工作负载优化从吞吐驱动转向延迟受限，现有张量并行方法无法满足需求。

Method: 提出CTA-pipelining延迟导向空间扩展技术，在Cooperative Thread Array级别利用依赖关系，实现依赖kernel跨GPU并发执行，充分挖掘共享内存多GPU系统的硬件特性。

Result: 在8-GPU H200和B200系统上基于CUTLASS、cuBLAS和NCCL库验证，在代表MLP操作的2层GEMM上，相比微批处理延迟降低31.8%，相比TP降低29.6%，且可与TP正交结合进一步降低延迟。

Conclusion: CTA-pipelining成功将多GPU共享内存硬件特性转化为延迟优势，为LLM推理延迟优化提供了新的扩展维度，可与现有TP技术正交结合，突破当前延迟边界。

Abstract: The evolution of compute infrastructure has transformed multi-GPU systems into tightly integrated shared-memory structures. However, current software still mostly treats these coherent interconnects simply as high-speed networks. Simultaneously, the demand for serving Large Language Models under latency constraints has shifted GPU workload optimization from being throughput-driven to latency-bound, necessitating latency-oriented scaling methods beyond Tensor Parallelism (TP).
  Thus, we introduce CTA-pipelining, an execution paradigm designed to exploit shared-memory multi-GPU systems. As a latency-oriented spatial scaling technique, CTA-pipelining leverages dependencies at the Cooperative Thread Array level, enabling concurrent execution of dependent kernels across GPUs. We demonstrate its capability using CUTLASS, cuBLAS, and NCCL libraries on 8-GPU H200 and B200 systems. Results show on 2-layer GEMM, representing the MLP operation, CTA-pipelining reduces latency by up to 31.8% compared to micro-batching, and 29.6% compared to TP. It can also be combined with TP as an orthogonal scaling dimension to further push the latency boundary.

</details>


### [162] [Toward a Unified GPU-Aware OpenSHMEM Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08006)
*Naveen Ravi,Nathan Wichmann,Md. Wasi-ur- Rahman,Aurelien Bouteiller,Yıltan Hassan Temuçin,Avinash Kethineedi,Johnathan Alsop,Brandon Potter,Shubhendra Pal Singhal,Jun Shirako,Akihiro Hayashi,Vivek Sarkar,Lawrence C. Stewart,Michael Beebe,Benjamin Michalowicz,Jeongnim Kim,Thiago Teixeria,Mark F. Brown,Aaron Welch,Oscar Hernandez,Wendy Poole,Steve Poole*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信辅助规范，通过GPU范围内存空间抽象和明确定义的语义，解决现有GPU-OpenSHMEM实现间的可移植性问题，实现跨厂商统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高性能计算系统以加速器为中心，但OpenSHMEM 1.x缺乏针对加速器架构的可移植内存模型语义。现有GPU-OpenSHMEM实现在内存管理、能力发现和操作语义上存在差异，限制了可移植性和生态系统凝聚力，存在分裂社区的风险。

Method: 提出OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信辅助规范，作为轻量级、向后兼容的扩展。引入GPU范围内存空间抽象的最小内存模型扩展，提供能力查询机制，并为GPU缓冲区的RMA、原子操作、同步和集合操作定义明确语义。设计以主机发起接口为主，同时支持可选的设备发起操作。

Result: 设计了一套GPU感知的OpenSHMEM语义规范，能够在多厂商GPU上实现和部署，为供应商中立规范的统一提供了实用且可快速实现的方案。

Conclusion: 该辅助规范为GPU感知OpenSHMEM语义提供了跨厂商可移植的基础，为未来OpenSHMEM规范设计提供参考，在保持向后兼容的同时有效解决社区分裂问题。

Abstract: Leadership-class HPC systems are now accelerator-centric, with GPUs providing most floating-point throughput and memory bandwidth. As next-generation systems increasingly integrate accelerators through high-speed memory fabrics and system interconnects, exposing larger tightly coupled device domains, \ac{PGAS} models such as OpenSHMEM provide a natural abstraction for expressing fine-grained remote memory operations across these devices. While OpenSHMEM 1.x offers a lean PGAS model for irregular communication, atomics, fine-grained synchronization, and collectives, its memory model lacks portable semantics for accelerator architectures. As a result, existing GPU-enabled OpenSHMEM implementations differ in memory management, capability discovery, and operation semantics, limiting portability and ecosystem cohesion. This risks fracturing the community that OpenSHMEM was originally created to unify.
  This paper proposes an OpenSHMEM Auxiliary Specification for GPU-Aware Communication, designed as a lightweight, backward-compatible extension to OpenSHMEM 1.x. The auxiliary specification introduces a minimal memory model extension via a GPU-scoped memory space abstraction, along with capability queries and well-defined semantics for using \acs{GPU}-attached buffers in RMA, atomic, synchronization, and collective operations. This is initially conceived through the lens of a host-initiated interface, although it provides a general set of semantics that also allow for optional device-initiated support. A central goal of this effort is to demonstrate that GPU-aware OpenSHMEM semantics can be specified and implemented across GPUs from multiple vendors, providing a practical and rapidly implementable step toward unification under a vendor-neutral specification while informing the design of future OpenSHMEM specifications.

</details>


### [163] [On the Limitations of Non-GPU AI Accelerators for Large-Model Inference: A Field Study of MoE and Multimodal Serving on Huawei Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08215)
*Zheng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于非GPU AI加速器部署的实地研究，记录了在华为昇腾910系统上部署两个大模型推理工作负载的工程挑战，发现了八大类平台局限性，并量化了集成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非GPU AI加速器正逐渐成为大模型推理的替代方案，但从CUDA迁移到新平台的实际工程成本和挑战缺乏充分文档记录。研究旨在填补这一空白，为评估和运营非GPU加速器的团队提供可复现的参考。

Method: 在16设备华为昇腾910系统上使用CANN和vLLM-Ascend部署两个实际工作负载：基于W8A8 MoE模型DeepSeek-V4-Flash的LLM安全与对齐评估流水线，以及基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision的多模态医学视觉-语言基准测试（MMMU和MMMU-Pro）。记录并分析部署过程中遇到的所有工程问题。

Result: 成功部署两个工作负载，但付出了显著的工程成本：需要对供应商推理插件进行12个源码级补丁，禁用多个高吞吐特性以保持数值正确性，并添加设备级故障的操作保障措施。识别出八大类平台局限性：算子和特性支持不完整、并行性脆弱、底层内核数值故障、图编译不成熟、高级特性不稳定、可扩展性有限、可观测性弱、生态系统碎片化。每个类别都报告了症状、证据和可能原因。

Conclusion: 非GPU加速器平台在可靠性方面仍存在显著挑战，涉及多个技术层面的局限性。本研究通过量化集成工作量、并发行为和基准质量，证明了工作负载的正确性，为团队评估或运营非GPU加速器提供了可复现的参考依据。

Abstract: Non-GPU AI accelerators are increasingly adopted as alternatives to general-purpose GPUs for large-model inference, but the real engineering cost of migrating demanding workloads beyond CUDA remains poorly documented. We present a field study of deploying two large inference workloads on a 16-device Huawei Ascend 910 system using CANN and vLLM-Ascend: an LLM-as-a-judge safety and alignment evaluation pipeline based on a W8A8 MoE judge model, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and a multimodal medical vision--language benchmark based on DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision for MMMU and MMMU-Pro. Making these workloads reliable required twelve source-level patches to the vendor inference plugin, disabling several high-throughput features to preserve numerical correctness, and adding operational safeguards for recurring device-level failures. We summarize the main platform limitations in eight categories: incomplete operator and feature support, fragile parallelism, numerical faults in low-level kernels, immature graph compilation, unstable advanced features, limited scalability, weak observability, and ecosystem fragmentation. For each category, we report the symptoms, evidence, and likely causes. We also quantify the integration effort, concurrency behavior, and benchmark quality to show that both workloads were served correctly. Our study provides a reproducible reference for teams evaluating or operating non-GPU accelerators for large-model inference.

</details>


### [164] [Self-Stabilizing Algorithms in the Uniform Port Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08244)
*Liam Brinker,Yuval Emek,Oren Louidor*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 首次在真正的均匀分布式计算模型（均匀端口模型）中，为极大独立集、极大匹配、无汇点定向等局部对称破缺问题设计了高效的自稳定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的真正均匀分布式计算模型（以节点为中心）无法自然表达半边标记问题（如极大匹配、无汇点定向），因此需要一种新的均匀模型来扩展表达能力，并在此模型中实现高效的自稳定算法。

Method: 提出均匀端口模型：在每个端口（半边）上托管一个相同的常数大小有限自动机。在该模型下，针对极大独立集、极大匹配、无汇点定向、极大节点/边k-染色等局部对称破缺问题，设计高效（多对数运行时间）的自稳定算法。

Result: 在一般图上，为极大独立集、极大匹配、无汇点定向、极大节点/边k-染色等基本局部对称破缺问题成功构造了高效的均匀端口自稳定算法。这是首次在真正均匀模型中证明此类算法的存在性。

Conclusion: 本工作填补了真正均匀分布式计算模型中高效自稳定算法的空白，扩展了均匀模型的表达能力，为局部对称破缺问题提供了新的算法设计范式。

Abstract: We introduce a distributed computational model referred to as the \emph{uniform port} model. An algorithm operating in this model is defined by means of local automata associated with the ports (a.k.a.\ half-edges) of the input graph. The crux of the uniform port model is that a single constant-size finite automaton is hosted by every port of every graph, making the model \emph{truly uniform}. Moreover, since the new model explicitly supports the assignment of (input and) output labels to the graph's (half-)edges, it facilitates natural formulations of (half-)edge-labeling problems such as maximal matching and sinkless orientation, which are outside the expressivity scope of prior node-centric truly uniform distributed computational models.
  The main technical contribution of this paper is the design of efficient (i.e., with poly-logarithmic runtime) \emph{self-stabilizing} uniform port algorithms, operating on general graphs, for various fundamental local symmetry breaking problems, including maximal independent set, maximal matching, sinkless orientation, and maximal node/edge $k$-coloring. While efficient self-stabilizing algorithms for local symmetry breaking problems have been extensively studied in stronger computational models, our work is the first to demonstrate the existence of such algorithms in a truly uniform model.

</details>


### [165] [Empirical Analysis of GPU Frequency Behavior Under ML Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08307)
*Truong-Thanh Le,Hoang-Loc La,Amir Taherkordi,Frank Eliassen,Phuong Hoai Ha,Peiyuan Guan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 发现低性能NVIDIA GPU执行ML/AI任务时存在频率缩放的时间依赖性，挑战了现有延迟预测技术的核心假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的ML延迟预测技术假设GPU内核延迟相互独立，通过累加孤立测量值估算总执行时间。作者发现这一假设可能不成立，需要研究GPU动态频率缩放对内核间依赖性的影响。

Method: 实验分析NVIDIA GPU在执行ML/AI工作负载时的频率缩放行为，重点观察低性能GPU在80ms时间窗口内的工作负载历史对运行频率的影响。

Result: 低性能GPU的运行频率受近期工作负载历史强烈影响（典型窗口为80ms），GPU动态频率缩放机制引入了内核间依赖性，证明现有技术将内核延迟视为独立的假设并不总是成立。

Conclusion: GPU动态频率缩放导致的内核间依赖性需要被纳入延迟预测模型，未来可在改进延迟预测模型、GPU内核重排序策略、以及频率/延迟/能耗感知的模型设计等方面展开研究。

Abstract: This work presents ongoing research on the frequency scaling behavior of NVIDIA GPUs when executing ML/AI workloads. Our preliminary findings show that, on lower-performance GPUs, the operating frequency is strongly affected by the recent workload history, typically within an 80ms window. This behavior challenges a common assumption underlying several state-of-the-art ML latency-prediction techniques, which treat individual GPU kernel latencies as independent and therefore estimate total execution time by summing isolated per-kernel measurements. Our results indicate that such an assumption does not always hold, as the GPU's dynamic frequency scaling introduces inter-kernel dependencies. We also outline several promising directions for leveraging this observation in future work, including improved latency-prediction models, GPU kernel-reordering strategies, and NAS-driven guidelines for frequency/latency/energy-aware model design.

</details>


### [166] [Adaptive Row Selection Meets Asynchrony in Randomized Kaczmarz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08313)
*Evan Coleman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 首次系统研究异步环境下随机化Kaczmarz算法的自适应行选择策略，发现线程数与采样激进程度存在稳定性边界，阈值贪婪策略在高并发下不稳定，欠松弛可提供安全调节，不一致读取反而比一致性快照读取更安全高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化Kaczmarz算法适用于大规模稀疏最小二乘和层析重建，自适应行选择可减少迭代次数。然而，在共享内存机器上部署自适应选择时，无锁工作线程需要从正在被并发修改的残差中采样，常使用过期数据。该工作旨在系统研究异步执行环境下残差加权和贪婪Kaczmarz的行为特征。

Method: 在96核节点上对异步执行下的残差加权Kaczmarz和贪婪Kaczmarz进行系统研究，共执行339次实验运行，测量真实延迟（非注入延迟）下的性能表现。验证实现与已发表的串行结果一致性，并设计分布式两层采样器。

Result: 四个核心发现：(i) 存在稳定性边界ℓ*(T)划分采样激进程度与线程计数，边界以下更激进的采样严格更优；(ii) 阈值贪婪选择（标准加速规则）在高线程数时不稳定，几乎立即发散；(iii) 欠松弛以可预测的代价换回稳定性边界，提供可用的安全调节机制；(iv) 一致性快照读取存在罕见的调度依赖性发散，而实时（不一致）读取从未出现此问题且成本更低。

Conclusion: 为异步共享内存环境下的自适应Kaczmarz算法提供了实践指导：应将参数调节至稳定性边界内、避免高并发下使用阈值贪婪策略、利用欠松弛作为安全调节、默认使用不一致读取。这些发现可直接应用于实践，并推动了分布式两层采样器的设计。

Abstract: Randomized Kaczmarz is a natural fit for large sparse least-squares and tomographic reconstruction, and adaptive row selection can reduce iteration counts. However, deploying adaptive selection on a shared-memory machine means sampling from a residual that lock-free workers are concurrently modifying, often using stale data. We present the first systematic study of this regime: residual-weighted and greedy Kaczmarz under asynchronous execution, measured across 339 runs on a 96-core node with realized (not injected) delays. Four findings carry directly to practice. (i) Stability is governed by a boundary $\ell^*(T)$ between sampling aggressiveness and thread count; below it, more aggressive sampling is strictly better, so one should tune to just inside the cliff. (ii) Threshold-greedy selection (the standard accelerated rule) is unstable at high thread counts, diverging almost immediately. (iii) Under-relaxation buys back the cliff at a predictable cost, giving a usable safety knob. (iv) Consistent-snapshot reads admit a rare, scheduling-dependent divergence that live (inconsistent) reads never exhibited and that is also cheaper, making inconsistent reads the right default. We validate the implementation against published sequential results and outline the distributed two-level sampler these measurements motivate.

</details>


### [167] [Computing in Anonymous Dynamic Networks with One-Bit Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08358)
*Thibaut Blanc,Giuseppe Antonio Di Luna,Giovanni Viglietta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在匿名动态网络中，即使每个代理每轮只广播1比特信息，仍能实现与对数级消息复杂度的拥塞模型相当的计算能力，并给出了紧致的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究匿名动态网络中确定性计算的理论极限，特别是在极端受限通信条件下（每轮仅1比特）的计算能力。此前已知在对数级消息复杂度的拥塞模型下可实现全局计算，本文旨在探索将消息压缩到单比特时是否仍能保持相似的计算能力。

Method: 提出了从局部1比特聚合观测中提取全局线性方程的方法：利用1比特割测试生成代理类的守恒约束，通过细化类别和收集独立约束来恢复所需多重性。对于未知网络规模的情况，引入了一种自适应洪泛原语。下界证明采用信息论方法，基于局部历史分析。

Result: 上界：有唯一领导者且已知网络规模上界U时，算法在O(n³log²n+U)轮内终止；未知n时，稳定算法运行时间为O(n³log²n)。下界：对于N=2^Ω(nlogn)的输入，复杂度为Ω(n³)轮，且该下界在有领导者、已知n和N、通信图为动态环等条件下仍成立。

Conclusion: 匿名动态网络的计算能力在对数级消息被压缩至1比特时基本保持不变，本文算法复杂度与拥塞模型的最优复杂度仅差对数因子，且下界证明该结果在渐近意义上近乎最优。

Abstract: We initiate the study of deterministic computation in anonymous dynamic networks where each agent broadcasts one bit per round and receives only the number of neighbors broadcasting each bit value. Despite this severe restriction, surprisingly rich global computation is possible.
  With a unique leader and a known upper bound $U$ on the network size $n$, we give a terminating algorithm for any computable function of the input multiset in $O(n^3\log^2 n+U)$ rounds, for inputs from a universe of size $N=2^{O(n\log n)}$. Without prior knowledge of $n$, we design a stabilizing algorithm for the same task running in $O(n^3\log^2 n)$ rounds. This essentially matches the state of the art for the congested model, where messages carry $O(\log n)$ bits and general computation takes $O(n^3)$ rounds. We also obtain comparable results for leaderless and multi-leader networks.
  We complement the upper bounds with an almost-matching lower bound of $$Ω!\left(\frac{n^2\log(N/n)}{\log n}\right)$$ rounds, which becomes $Ω(n^3)$ for $N=2^{Ω(n\log n)}$. The proof is information-theoretic, based on local histories, and holds even with a unique leader, known $n$ and $N$, and a communication graph restricted to a dynamically changing ring.
  Our algorithms extract global linear equations from local one-bit aggregate observations. A one-bit cut test yields conservation constraints on the sizes of indistinguishable agent classes; by refining these classes and collecting independent constraints, agents recover the required multiplicities. For unknown size, we introduce a self-correcting adaptive flooding primitive of independent interest. Thus, the computational power of congested anonymous dynamic networks is essentially preserved even when every message is compressed to one bit.

</details>


### [168] [Coded Task Offloading for Fluid Computing: A Privacy-Aware Approach under D2D Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08440)
*Diego Cajaraville-Aboy,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Ana Fernández-Vilas,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向D2D网络的编码任务卸载方案，结合线性秘密共享技术，在随机任务到达和队列动态下实现延迟-能耗-隐私的三方权衡优化，启发式算法性能接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务卸载方案很少考虑对抗执行环境下的隐私风险和信息泄露问题，且大多数编码计算方案仅关注滞后节点缓解而忽略能耗等系统级目标，缺乏在动态、隐私敏感环境中自适应的任务执行机制。

Method: 提出了一种编码任务卸载方案，将任务卸载技术与线性秘密共享方案相结合，将任务编码为冗余分片以支持基于阈值的恢复、滞后节点缓解和隐私保护。形式化隐私感知卸载问题，联合优化延迟和能耗，同时对噪声泄露观察下的理论隐私泄露进行惩罚。采用分支定界求解器和轻量级启发式调度器求解，通过离散事件模拟器进行评估。

Result: 编码卸载在延迟-能耗权衡方面优于传统的全卸载和并行卸载方案；启发式算法实现近最优性能，优于基线和最先进求解器；隐私泄露惩罚重塑卸载决策，揭示了延迟-能耗-隐私的内在三方权衡关系。

Conclusion: 通过将编码计算与隐私保护机制相结合，所提方案能够在D2D网络中有效平衡延迟、能耗和隐私三方面目标，为流体计算环境下的隐私感知任务卸载提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Fluid Computing aims to support distributed applications execution across heterogeneous cloud, edge, and device resources, motivating task execution mechanisms that adapt to dynamic and privacy-sensitive environments under runtime conditions. In this context, current task offloading schemes rarely address privacy risks and information leakage under adversarial execution settings; furthermore, most coded computing proposals focus on straggler mitigation without considering system-level objectives such as energy awareness. This paper proposes a coded task offloading scheme for D2D networks under stochastic task arrivals and queue-based dynamics. The proposal combines task offloading techniques with linear secret sharing schemes, where tasks are encoded into redundant shares to support threshold-based recovery, straggler mitigation, and privacy preservation while enhancing system performance. Then, we formulate a privacy-aware offloading problem that jointly optimizes delay and energy while penalizing the theoretical privacy leakage of coded tasks under noisy leakage observations. The problem is solved using a branch-and-bound solver alongside a lightweight heuristic scheduler, both of which are evaluated through a discrete-event simulator. Results show that coded offloading improves the delay--energy trade-off with respect to classical full and parallel offloading schemes, while the heuristic achieves near-optimal performance, outperforming baseline and state-of-the-art solvers. The results also show how privacy leakage penalties reshape offloading decisions, exposing an inherent delay--energy--privacy trade-off.

</details>


### [169] [SMetric: Rethink LLM Scheduling for Serving Agents with Balanced Session-centric Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08565)
*Jiahao Wang,Kaizhan Lin,Kaixi Zhang,Jinbo Han,Xingda Wei,Sijie Shen,Chenguang Fang,Wenyuan Yu,Rong Chen,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SMETRIC调度器，通过将会话的首个请求用于负载均衡、后续请求保持缓存感知的策略，在Agent服务场景下实现了集群吞吐量提升10-16%，同时改善了每token延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度设计主要面向人类用户，而Agent服务场景具有不同特征：（1）Agent仅对完整响应采取行动，使集群TPS成为主要优化目标；（2）请求间KV$共享率高达80%（远超聊天场景的54-62%）。现有调度器为提升KV$重用率，过度优先路由到缓存实例，导致少数实例过载而其他闲置，限制了TPS。

Method: SMETRIC采用平衡会话中心调度策略：（1）将会话首个请求纯粹用于负载均衡，后续请求采用缓存感知方式路由；（2）利用工作负载的会话内局部性，仅需平衡小部分请求即可实现集群负载均衡；（3）使用会话轮次信息作为调度指标，该指标可从用户输入高效准确推导，保持调度器无状态特性。

Result: 在预填充-解码协同部署且配备全局存储的场景下，SMETRIC相比最先进调度器将集群TPS提升10-16%；在分离部署场景下将预填充TPS提升2-34%，同时获得更好的每token延迟性能。

Conclusion: SMETRIC通过会话中心调度策略有效解决了Agent服务场景下的负载均衡与KV$重用之间的矛盾。该方法无需牺牲大部分本地KV$重用即可实现负载均衡，降低对全局存储的需求，且调度器保持简洁无状态的设计特性。

Abstract: LLM scheduling is critical to serving, yet it remains unclear how well existing designs fit agentic serving--with LLM requests issued by agents instead of humans. This shifts the workload in two ways: (1) agents act only on complete responses, making the cluster's tokens per second (TPS) the primary goal and relaxing--not eliminating--per-token latency requirements; and (2) requests share much of their KV\$-reuse exceeds 80% of request tokens in a production trace from BAILIAN, versus 54-62% in chat.
  This paper first contributes a systematic study of request scheduling for agents on two real-world traces. We find that to increase KV\$ reuse, existing schedulers overly prioritize routing requests to instances caching their KV\$, overloading a few while leaving the rest idle, capping TPS. We thus present two key insights: (1) load balance need not sacrifice all KV\$ reuse, thanks to the global-tier KV\$ store and (2) by utilizing the workload's intra-session locality, balancing a small fraction of requests--the first request in each agent session--suffices to balance the cluster without sacrificing most KV\$ reuse on local instances.
  SMETRIC realizes these insights with balanced session-centric scheduling: it routes each session's first request purely for load balance and its follow-up requests in a cache-aware manner, preserving load balance and local reuse while keeping demand on the global tier low. Using the session turn information as the scheduling metric is deliberate: it is derived efficiently and accurately from the user inputs alone, so the scheduler stays clean and stateless. SMETRIC improves cluster TPS by 10-16% under prefill-decode colocation with a global store and prefill TPS by 2-34% under disaggregation over state-of-the-art schedulers, also with a better per-token latency.

</details>
